# Content inventory

Read-only inventory after Phase 2a. Template claims are not facts about Revanth. Sources are matched against served files; `captured/` was not modified. Both phone and desktop DOMs were sampled, including off-screen and hover/reveal content. Identical strings are deduplicated per location within each route. Removed pricing/promotion content is preserved in the baseline appendix.

GitHub data remains only in `tools/github-data.json`; it is not used by the site. The supplied pins have not replaced the template projects.

Still pending: stock project case studies and metrics, fake testimonials (explicitly retained), old designer/client/service/about/FAQ copy, dates, technology-logo placeholders, social links other than GitHub, and contact Budget options. No manifest or JSON-LD exists in the captured served pages.

## Route /

Static source: `index.html`. Runtime and CMS sources are listed for each matching string/asset.
